N; const x = 16 * Math.pow(Math.sin(t), 3); const y = 13 * Math.cos(t) - 5 * Math.cos(2 * t) - 2 * Math.cos(3 * t) - Math.cos(4 * t); const img = document.createElement("img"); img.className = "heartPic"; img.alt = ""; img.src = albumPhotos[i % albumPhotos.length]; img.style.left = (50 + (x / 16) * 38) + "%"; img.style.top = (50 - ((y + 2.5) / 14.5) * 38) + "%"; img.style.transform = `translate(-50%,-50%) rotate(${(i % 2 ? 1 : -1) * rnd(4, 12)}deg)`; img.style.animationDelay = (Math.min(i, N - i) * 0.09) + "s"; box.appendChild(img); } const main = document.createElement("img"); main.className = "heartPic main"; main.alt = ""; main.src = albumPhotos[0]; main.style.left = "50%"; main.style.top = "41%"; main.style.transform = "translate(-50%,-50%)"; main.style.animationDelay = "1.5s"; box.appendChild(main); } /* LETTER */ const wishOverlay = document.getElementById("wishOverlay"); let wishTimers = []; function playWish() { wishTimers.forEach(clearTimeout); wishTimers = []; wishOverlay.querySelectorAll(".wishLine").forEach((el, i) => { el.classList.remove("show"); wishTimers.push(setTimeout(() => el.classList.add("show"), 400 + i * 1100)); }); } document.getElementById("wishBtn").addEventListener("click", () => { albumOverlay.classList.remove("active"); wishOverlay.classList.add("active"); playWish(); }); document.getElementById("wishBack").addEventListener("click", () => { wishTimers.forEach(clearTimeout); wishOverlay.classList.remove("active"); albumOverlay.classList.add("active"); });
